# Backend environment setup report

All shell commands ran from /Users/prest/Documents/ChatGPT/expedia-lite.
No application code, routes, or tests were written. No global packages or machine settings were changed.
The existing backend/.venv was preserved and refreshed with the compatible Python 3.14.7.

## Every shell command run

### 1. Read project instructions and inspect Python (exit 0)
```sh
cat AGENTS.md README.md backend/requirements.txt
ls -ld backend/.venv
cat backend/.venv/pyvenv.cfg
/Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/bin/python3 --version
```
Evidence: existing virtual environment; include-system-site-packages = false; Python 3.14.7; base interpreter /Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/bin/python3.14.

### 2. Record the initial file inventory (exit 0)
```sh
/Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/bin/python3 -B - <<'PY'
import json
from pathlib import Path
root = Path.cwd()
paths = sorted(str(p.relative_to(root)) for p in root.rglob('*') if '.git' not in p.relative_to(root).parts and 'node_modules' not in p.relative_to(root).parts)
Path('backend/environment-before.json').write_text(json.dumps(paths, indent=2) + '\n')
print('Saved pre-install file inventory to backend/environment-before.json')
PY
```

### 3. Ensure the virtual environment and install requirements
```sh
/Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/bin/python3 -m venv backend/.venv
backend/.venv/bin/python -m pip --isolated --disable-pip-version-check --no-cache-dir install -r backend/requirements.txt
```
The environment command completed. The installation exited 1 because the sandbox could not resolve pypi.org. This was a network failure, not evidence of an unavailable pytest release.

### 4. Retry the installation with approved network access (exit 0)
```sh
backend/.venv/bin/python -m pip --isolated --disable-pip-version-check --no-cache-dir install -r backend/requirements.txt
```
Only the two declared requirements and their transitive dependencies/extras were requested. Existing satisfied packages were retained. No sudo or global installation was used.
Installed: MarkupSafe 3.0.3, agent-detector 2.0.0, certifi 2026.7.22, detect-installer 0.2.1, dnspython 2.8.0, email-validator 2.3.0, fastapi-cli 0.0.32, fastapi-cloud-cli 0.26.0, fastar 0.12.0, httpcore 1.0.9, httpx 0.28.1, iniconfig 2.3.0, jinja2 3.1.6, markdown-it-py 4.2.0, mdurl 0.1.2, packaging 26.3, pluggy 1.6.0, pydantic-extra-types 2.11.1, pydantic-settings 2.15.0, pygments 2.21.0, pytest 9.1.1, python-multipart 0.0.32, rich 15.0.0, rich-toolkit 0.20.5, rignore 0.8.1, sentry-sdk 2.69.1, shellingham 1.5.4, typer 0.27.2, urllib3 2.7.0.

### 5. Verify environment ownership, imports, and dependency consistency (exit 0)
```sh
backend/.venv/bin/python -B - <<'PY'
import sys
from pathlib import Path
import fastapi
import pytest
expected = Path('backend/.venv').absolute()
assert Path(sys.executable).parent == expected / 'bin'
assert Path(sys.prefix) == expected
assert sys.prefix != sys.base_prefix
print('Interpreter:', sys.executable)
print('Python:', sys.version.split()[0])
print('Environment prefix:', sys.prefix)
print('Base prefix:', sys.base_prefix)
for module in (fastapi, pytest):
    assert Path(module.__file__).is_relative_to(expected)
    print(module.__name__, module.__version__, module.__file__)
print('PASS: interpreter and both imports belong to backend/.venv')
PY
backend/.venv/bin/python -B -m pip --isolated --disable-pip-version-check check
```

### 6. Generate the complete created-path inventory
```sh
/Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/bin/python3 -B - <<'PY'
import json
from pathlib import Path
root = Path.cwd()
before = set(json.loads(Path('backend/environment-before.json').read_text()))
after = {str(p.relative_to(root)) for p in root.rglob('*') if '.git' not in p.relative_to(root).parts and 'node_modules' not in p.relative_to(root).parts}
created = sorted(after - before)
with Path('backend/environment-setup-report.md').open('a') as report:
    report.write('\n## Every created file or directory\n\nPaths are relative to the project root. Existing .venv and requirements.txt were updated, not newly created.\n\n')
    for item in created:
        suffix = '/' if Path(item).is_dir() else ''
        report.write('- ' + item + suffix + '\n')
print('Created paths recorded:', len(created))
print('Full inventory: backend/environment-setup-report.md')
PY
```

## File edits made through apply_patch (not shell commands)

- Updated backend/requirements.txt to exactly:
```text
fastapi[standard]
pytest
```
- Updated README.md to remove its stale installation-state assertion and describe backend requirements and virtual-environment use.
- Created this report, backend/environment-setup-report.md.
- The inventory command appends the created paths to this report.
